```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1s.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: k1s.h_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 ordinary business income (loss), zero if box 1 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 net rental real estate income (loss), zero if box 2 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 other net rental income (loss), zero if box 3 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (loss), zero if box 7 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box7
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b8a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (loss), zero if boxes 8a-8c are blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b8b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) gain (loss), zero if boxes 8a-8c are blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b1_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income (zero if a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b1, take: k1s.h_zero}

  - id: k1s.h_b1_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss as a positive amount (zero if income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b1}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income (zero if a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b2, take: k1s.h_zero}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss as a positive amount (zero if income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b2}

  - id: k1s.h_b3_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 income (zero if a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b3, take: k1s.h_zero}

  - id: k1s.h_b3_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss as a positive amount (zero if income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b3}

  - id: k1s.h_n7203
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 7203 for this corporation"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.one}

  - id: k1s.h_a35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 35, column (a): ordinary business loss reported"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.a35}

  - id: k1s.h_c35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 35, column (c): ordinary business loss allowed by stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c35}

  - id: k1s.h_a36
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 36, column (a): net rental real estate loss reported"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.a36}

  - id: k1s.h_c36
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 36, column (c): net rental real estate loss allowed by stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c36}

  - id: k1s.h_a37
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 37, column (a): other net rental loss reported"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.a37}

  - id: k1s.h_c37
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 37, column (c): other net rental loss allowed by stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c37}

  - id: k1s.h_a38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 38, column (a): net capital loss reported"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.a38}

  - id: k1s.h_c38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 38, column (c): net capital loss allowed by stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c38}
    note: "The i7203 text gives Part III's columns but not the item on each line. Lines 35-38 are read from the catalog's markers as ordinary business, net rental real estate, other net rental and net capital loss."

  - id: k1s.h_b1_allow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss allowed after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.h_b1_neg, k1s.h_c35]}
    note: "Basis limits apply first (i1120ssk p. 6). The allowed loss is taken from Form 7203 Part III column (c). Debt basis (column (d)) is not in the catalog. It comes into play only when stock basis is used up, which the basis-limited check refuses for QBI. Loans are handled on Form 7203."

  - id: k1s.h_b2_allow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss allowed after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.h_b2_neg, k1s.h_c36]}

  - id: k1s.h_b3_allow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss allowed after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.h_b3_neg, k1s.h_c37]}

  - id: k1s.h_any_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Does this Schedule K-1 report a loss in box 1, 2, 3, 7, 8a or 8b?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b1, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b2, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b3, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b7, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b8a, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b8b, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.h_7203_missing
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss is claimed but there is not exactly one Form 7203 for this corporation"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [k1s.h_n7203, {const: 1, type: Count}]}

  - id: k1s.h_risk
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss is reported and amounts may not be at risk (Form 6198)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_cap_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is the net capital loss limited by basis (Form 7203 line 38, column (c) less than column (a))?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c38, k1s.h_a38]}

  - id: k1s.h_basis_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is any box 1-3 loss limited by stock basis?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c35, k1s.h_a35]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c36, k1s.h_a36]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c37, k1s.h_a37]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c38, k1s.h_a38]}

  - id: k1s.h_screen
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Situations this tool does not compute for this Schedule K-1"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.multi, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.disposed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.fiscal, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box6, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box9, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box11_12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box14, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box15, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16ef, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.basis_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.risk_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.credit_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.interest_buy, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_material
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Did you materially participate (tests 1-7) in the corporation's activity?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.work, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.hours500, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.most_hours, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.mp_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.past_years, {const: true}]}
    note: "Test 5 or 6 (prior-year participation) can be met without any 2025 work. The other tests need 2025 work."

  - id: k1s.h_b1_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 is nonpassive (material participation or trading for owners)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_material, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.trader, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b1_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 is passive"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is nonpassive (real estate professional who materially participated, or personal-use dwelling)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.re_pro, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_material, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: true}]}
    note: "The dwelling-unit rule makes rental amounts nonpassive (i1120ssk p. 5, special rules). It is applied to boxes 2 and 3."

  - id: k1s.h_b2_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is passive"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b3_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 is nonpassive (personal-use dwelling)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 is passive"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_mfs_together
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and lived with spouse at any time during the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [household.mfs_living, {const: "apart_all_year"}]}

  - id: k1s.h_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 passive activity with active participation (Form 8582 Part IV)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mfs_together, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 passive activity without active participation (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b1_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 1 activity (zero if none)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b1_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 2 activity (zero if none)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b2_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 3 activity (zero if none)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b3_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_prior_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A prior-year unallowed loss belongs to an activity that is now nonpassive or has no 2025 amount"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b1_prior, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b2_prior, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b3_prior, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: false}]}
    note: "The text does not cover a prior-year loss of a former passive activity, or of an activity with no 2025 amount. These cases are refused rather than dropped."

  - id: k1s.h_p4_prior_on
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year box 2 loss goes in Form 8582 Part IV column (c) (active participation in both years)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_not_active, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_prior_on
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year box 2 loss goes in Form 8582 Part V column (c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4_prior_on, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1s.h_b1_np_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 1 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b1_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_np_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 2 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_np_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 3 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b3_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b1_np_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 1 loss allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b1_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_np_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 2 loss allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_np_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 3 loss allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b3_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b1_pass_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passive box 1 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b1_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_pass_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passive box 2 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_pass_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passive box 3 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b3_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Nonpassive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_np_inc, k1s.h_b2_np_inc, k1s.h_b3_np_inc]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]
    note: "Nonpassive income and losses are kept in separate columns (k) and (i) rather than netted across boxes."

  - id: k1s.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Nonpassive loss allowed (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_np_loss, k1s.h_b2_np_loss, k1s.h_b3_np_loss]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk]

  - id: k1s.col_h
    printed: "28 (h)"
    label: "Passive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_pass_inc, k1s.h_b2_pass_inc, k1s.h_b3_pass_inc]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.p4_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV column (a): current-year net income of the box 2 activity with active participation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.p4_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV column (b): current-year net loss of the box 2 activity with active participation, after basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk]

  - id: k1s.p4_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV column (c): prior-year unallowed loss of the box 2 activity (active participation both years)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4_prior_on, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4_prior_on, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_prior_refuse]

  - id: k1s.h_b1_p5_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V income from the box 1 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b1_pass_inc}

  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V income from the box 2 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V column (a): current-year net income of passive activities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_p5_inc, k1s.h_b2_p5_inc, k1s.h_b3_pass_inc]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.h_b1_p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V loss from the box 1 activity, after basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b1_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V loss from the box 2 activity, after basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V loss from the box 3 activity, after basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b3_allow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V column (b): current-year net loss of passive activities, after basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_p5_loss, k1s.h_b2_p5_loss, k1s.h_b3_p5_loss]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk]

  - id: k1s.h_b1_p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V prior-year unallowed loss of the box 1 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b1_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V prior-year unallowed loss of the box 2 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5_prior_on, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b2_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5_prior_on, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3_p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V prior-year unallowed loss of the box 3 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_b3_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b3_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V column (c): prior-year unallowed losses of passive activities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_p5_prior, k1s.h_b2_p5_prior, k1s.h_b3_p5_prior]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_prior_refuse]

  - id: k1s.nonpassive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net nonpassive income (loss) reported on Schedule E line 28 (column (k) minus column (i))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.col_k, take: k1s.col_i}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk]

  - id: k1s.int
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4 interest income (Form 1040, line 2b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box4
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.ord_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5a ordinary dividends (Form 1040, line 3b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box5a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.qual_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5b qualified dividends (Form 1040, line 3a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box5b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.st
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 5)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b7}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk, k1s.h_cap_limited]
    note: "Capital losses are reported as shown only when Form 7203 line 38 allows them in full. A basis-limited capital loss is refused rather than split between boxes 7 and 8a."

  - id: k1s.lt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 12)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b8a}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk, k1s.h_cap_limited]

  - id: k1s.g28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) gain (loss) (28% Rate Gain Worksheet, line 4)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b8b}
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_7203_missing, k1s.h_risk, k1s.h_cap_limited]

  - id: k1s.u1250
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8c unrecaptured section 1250 gain from the corporation's business assets (Unrecaptured Section 1250 Gain Worksheet, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8c
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.box8_stmt]

  - id: k1s.backup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 13 code O backup withholding (Form 1040, line 25c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box13o
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]

  - id: k1s.taxexempt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16 code A tax-exempt interest income (Form 1040, line 2a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box16a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen]
    note: "Only code A is reported on line 2a. Code B other tax-exempt income is not included in income."

  - id: k1s.h_v_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Code V statement shows section 199A dividends or PTP income"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.v_other, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_qbi_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "QBI items may include losses not allowed this year (basis or passive limits) or prior-year losses allowed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_basis_limited, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.p4_loss, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.p5_loss, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.p4_prior, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.p5_prior, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 17 code V qualified business income (loss)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.qbi_stmt
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_screen, k1s.h_v_other, k1s.h_qbi_limited]
    note: "Only items allowed in figuring taxable income count as QBI (i1120ssk code V). The statement amount is used as reported. Cases where a loss is limited by basis or the passive rules, or a prior-year loss is allowed, are refused."
```
